## Round 1: Fruit, Patterns and Shapes

`round-001` · Grade 5 / CogAT Level 11 · 15 questions

**Q1 · ODD ONE OUT** · verbal · easy · 5s _(needs-human-check)_

- Prompt: WHICH ONE DOES NOT BELONG?
  - A) APPLE
  - B) BANANA
  - C) CARROT ✅
  - D) GRAPE
- Correct: C · CARROT
- Explanation: An apple, banana, and grape are all fruits. A carrot is a vegetable, so it does not belong.

**Q2 · NUMBER SERIES** · quantitative · easy · 5s

- Prompt: WHAT COMES NEXT?  —  sequence: 5, 10, 15, 20, ?
  - A) 25 ✅
  - B) 30
  - C) 21
  - D) 24
- Correct: A · 25
- Explanation: Each number goes up by 5: 5, 10, 15, 20, so the next is 20 plus 5 is 25.

**Q3 · FIGURE ANALOGY** · nonverbal · easy · 5s

- Prompt: WHICH SHAPE COMPLETES THE PATTERN?  —  empty circle : filled circle  =  empty triangle : ?
  - A) filled circle
  - B) empty triangle
  - C) filled square
  - D) filled triangle ✅
- Correct: D · FILLED TRIANGLE
- Explanation: The rule is the shape gets filled in but stays the same shape, so the empty triangle becomes a filled triangle.

**Q4 · VERBAL ANALOGY** · verbal · easy · 5s _(needs-human-check)_

- Prompt: HOT IS TO COLD AS / DAY IS TO ?
  - A) MORNING
  - B) NIGHT ✅
  - C) SUN
  - D) LIGHT
- Correct: B · NIGHT
- Explanation: Hot and cold are opposites, so the opposite of day is night. It is the same relationship, flipped.

**Q5 · NUMBER SERIES** · quantitative · easy · 5s

- Prompt: WHAT COMES NEXT?  —  sequence: 2, 4, 8, 16, ?
  - A) 24
  - B) 20
  - C) 32 ✅
  - D) 30
- Correct: C · 32
- Explanation: Each number doubles: 2, 4, 8, 16, so the next is 16 times 2 is 32.

**Q6 · VERBAL ANALOGY** · verbal · medium · 5s _(needs-human-check)_

- Prompt: BEE IS TO HIVE AS / BIRD IS TO ?
  - A) NEST ✅
  - B) TREE
  - C) EGG
  - D) SKY
- Correct: A · NEST
- Explanation: A bee lives in a hive, so a bird lives in a nest. Each one is the home that animal builds.

**Q7 · NUMBER SERIES** · quantitative · hard · 5s

- Prompt: WHAT COMES NEXT?  —  sequence: 1, 2, 5, 10, 17, ?
  - A) 24
  - B) 26 ✅
  - C) 25
  - D) 28
- Correct: B · 26
- Explanation: The gaps are the odd numbers 1, 3, 5, 7, then 9. So 17 plus 9 is 26.

**Q8 · VERBAL ANALOGY** · verbal · easy · 5s _(needs-human-check)_

- Prompt: FISH IS TO SWIM AS / BIRD IS TO ?
  - A) WALK
  - B) SING
  - C) HOP
  - D) FLY ✅
- Correct: D · FLY
- Explanation: A fish moves by swimming, so a bird moves by flying. It is how each animal gets around.

**Q9 · NUMBER SERIES** · quantitative · medium · 5s

- Prompt: WHAT COMES NEXT?  —  sequence: 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, ?
  - A) 18
  - B) 20
  - C) 21 ✅
  - D) 26
- Correct: C · 21
- Explanation: Add the two numbers before it: 8 plus 13 is 21. It is the add-the-last-two pattern.

**Q10 · ODD ONE OUT** · verbal · medium · 5s _(needs-human-check)_

- Prompt: WHICH ONE DOES NOT BELONG?
  - A) PIANO
  - B) GUITAR
  - C) DRUM
  - D) SONG ✅
- Correct: D · SONG
- Explanation: A piano, guitar, and drum are all instruments you play. A song is the music they make, so it does not belong.

**Q11 · FIGURE SERIES** · nonverbal · medium · 5s

- Prompt: WHICH SHAPE COMES NEXT?  —  sides: 6, 5, 4, ?
  - A) triangle (3 sides) ✅
  - B) square (4 sides)
  - C) circle
  - D) pentagon (5 sides)
- Correct: A · TRIANGLE
- Explanation: The sides count down by one: hexagon 6, pentagon 5, square 4, so next is the triangle with 3 sides.

**Q12 · SENTENCE COMPLETION** · verbal · hard · 5s _(needs-human-check)_

- Prompt: THE LIBRARY WAS SO QUIET THAT EVEN A / WHISPER SEEMED ______, SO EVERYONE / TRIED HARD NOT TO MAKE A SOUND.
  - A) HEAVY
  - B) LOUD ✅
  - C) COLORFUL
  - D) EXPENSIVE
- Correct: B · LOUD
- Explanation: In a place that quiet, even a tiny whisper stands out and seems loud. Only loud fits the meaning.

**Q13 · POSITION** · nonverbal · medium · 5s

- Prompt: WHERE DOES THE DOT MOVE NEXT?  —  path: bottom-left, top-left, top-right, ?
  - A) top-left
  - B) bottom-right ✅
  - C) center
  - D) bottom-left
- Correct: B · BOTTOM-RIGHT
- Explanation: The dot steps clockwise corner to corner: bottom-left, top-left, top-right, so the next corner is bottom-right.

**Q14 · NUMBER ANALOGY** · quantitative · medium · 5s

- Prompt: WHICH NUMBER FITS? / 2 -> 3,   3 -> 5,   4 -> 7,   5 -> ?
  - A) 8
  - B) 10
  - C) 11
  - D) 9 ✅
- Correct: D · 9
- Explanation: Each number is doubled then take away one: 5 times 2 is 10, minus 1 is 9.

**Q15 · NUMBER PUZZLE** · quantitative · hard · 5s

- Prompt: IF  2+3=10,  4+1=10,  5+2=14 / THEN  3+4 = ?
  - A) 7
  - B) 12
  - C) 14 ✅
  - D) 24
- Correct: C · 14
- Explanation: The trick is to add the two numbers, then double: 3 plus 4 is 7, and 7 times 2 is 14.

  - A) 8
  - B) 14
  - C) 12 ✅
  - D) 16
- Correct: C · 12
- Explanation: The plus sign secretly means multiply: 2 times 3 is 6, 4 times 2 is 8, so 6 times 2 is 12.

  - B) 9
  - C) 20
  - D) 1
- Correct: A · 11
- Explanation: Multiply the two numbers, then subtract them both: 5 times 4 is 20, minus 5 minus 4 leaves 11.
